         SUM(CASE WHEN usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN size_in_bytes ELSE 0 END) AS unread_bytes,
         SUM(reads) AS reads, SUM(writes) AS writes, MAX(last_read) AS last_read, MAX(last_write) AS last_write,
         COUNT_IF(sensitive_columns > 0) AS sensitive_tables
  FROM c WHERE workspace_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1
),
-- Columns per table and view.
cols AS (
  SELECT table_catalog, table_schema, table_name, COUNT(*) AS n FROM system.information_schema.columns
  WHERE table_schema <> 'information_schema' GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
),
-- Per workspace: catalogs, tables, views and their columns, of the catalogs it is home to.
held AS (
  SELECT p.workspace_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.catalog) AS catalogs,
         COUNT_IF(t.table_type <> 'VIEW') AS tables, COUNT_IF(t.table_type = 'VIEW') AS views,
         COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN t.table_type <> 'VIEW' THEN cl.n END), 0) AS table_columns,
         COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN t.table_type = 'VIEW' THEN cl.n END), 0) AS view_columns
  FROM place p
  LEFT JOIN system.information_schema.tables t ON t.table_catalog = p.catalog AND t.table_schema <> 'information_schema'
  LEFT JOIN cols cl ON cl.table_catalog = t.table_catalog AND cl.table_schema = t.table_schema AND cl.table_name = t.table_name
  WHERE p.workspace_id IS NOT NULL
  GROUP BY 1
),
-- Per workspace: volumes, and their files and bytes from the job's volume_sizes.
vols AS (
  SELECT p.workspace_id, COUNT(*) AS volumes, COALESCE(SUM(vs.files), 0) AS volume_files,
         COALESCE(SUM(vs.size_in_bytes), 0) AS volume_bytes
  FROM system.information_schema.volumes v
  JOIN place p ON p.catalog = v.volume_catalog
  LEFT JOIN main.data_usage.volume_sizes vs
    ON vs.catalog = v.volume_catalog AND vs.schema = v.volume_schema AND vs.volume_name = v.volume_name
  WHERE p.workspace_id IS NOT NULL
  GROUP BY 1
),
-- Per workspace: bytes of its tables, from the job's table_sizes (DESCRIBE DETAIL).
sizes AS (
  SELECT p.workspace_id, SUM(s.size_in_bytes) AS table_bytes
  FROM main.data_usage.table_sizes s JOIN place p ON p.catalog = s.catalog
  WHERE p.workspace_id IS NOT NULL
  GROUP BY 1
),
-- Audit events and queries in the window, per workspace, identity and day.
ev AS (
  -- people and service principals seen in the window: an audit event or a query
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, lower(COALESCE(user_identity.email, user_identity.subject_name)) AS who,
         event_date AS d, event_time AS ts, 0 AS is_query
  FROM system.access.audit WHERE event_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  UNION ALL
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING), lower(executed_by), CAST(start_time AS DATE), start_time, 1
  FROM system.query.history WHERE start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) AND start_time < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
),
-- Per workspace: active people, service principals, queries, active days and the last query.
act AS (
  SELECT workspace_id,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN who LIKE '%@%' THEN who END) AS active_people,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN who RLIKE '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' THEN who END) AS active_service_principals,
         SUM(is_query) AS queries,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN who LIKE '%@%' OR who RLIKE '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' THEN d END) AS active_days,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_query = 1 THEN ts END) AS last_query
  FROM ev GROUP BY 1
),
-- Every workspace with activity or a catalog.
ids AS (
  SELECT workspace_id FROM act UNION SELECT workspace_id FROM held
),
-- Everything per workspace; a missing count reads 0.
base AS (
  SELECT i.workspace_id, COALESCE(w.workspace_name, i.workspace_id) AS workspace, w.workspace_url,
         COALESCE(h.catalogs, 0) AS catalogs, COALESCE(h.tables, 0) AS tables, COALESCE(h.views, 0) AS views,
         COALESCE(h.table_columns, 0) AS table_columns, COALESCE(h.view_columns, 0) AS view_columns,
         COALESCE(v.volumes, 0) AS volumes, COALESCE(v.volume_files, 0) AS volume_files,
         COALESCE(s.table_bytes, 0) AS table_bytes, COALESCE(s.table_bytes, 0) + COALESCE(v.volume_bytes, 0) AS data_bytes,
         COALESCE(a.active_people, 0) AS active_people, COALESCE(a.active_service_principals, 0) AS active_service_principals,
         COALESCE(a.queries, 0) AS queries, COALESCE(a.active_days, 0) AS active_days, a.last_query,
         COALESCE(k.tables_written_never_read, 0) AS tables_written_never_read, COALESCE(k.tables_idle, 0) AS tables_idle,
         COALESCE(k.tables_cooling, 0) AS tables_cooling, COALESCE(k.tables_active, 0) AS tables_active,
         COALESCE(k.unread_bytes, 0) AS unread_bytes, COALESCE(k.reads, 0) AS reads, COALESCE(k.writes, 0) AS writes,
         k.last_read, k.last_write, COALESCE(k.sensitive_tables, 0) AS sensitive_tables
  FROM ids i
  LEFT JOIN held h ON h.workspace_id = i.workspace_id
  LEFT JOIN vols v ON v.workspace_id = i.workspace_id
  LEFT JOIN sizes s ON s.workspace_id = i.workspace_id
  LEFT JOIN act a ON a.workspace_id = i.workspace_id
  LEFT JOIN cls k ON k.workspace_id = i.workspace_id
  LEFT JOIN wsl w ON w.workspace_id = i.workspace_id
  WHERE i.workspace_id IS NOT NULL AND i.workspace_id <> '0'
),
-- The complexity and the usage index (app/core/usage.py), each input on a log scale against the largest workspace shown.
idx AS (
  SELECT *,
         ROUND(100 * (0.2 * COALESCE(ln(1 + views) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + views)) OVER (), 0), 0) + 0.15 * COALESCE(ln(1 + view_columns) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + view_columns)) OVER (), 0), 0) + 0.2 * COALESCE(ln(1 + tables) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + tables)) OVER (), 0), 0) + 0.15 * COALESCE(ln(1 + table_columns) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + table_columns)) OVER (), 0), 0) + 0.1 * COALESCE(ln(1 + volume_files) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + volume_files)) OVER (), 0), 0) + 0.2 * COALESCE(ln(1 + table_bytes) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + table_bytes)) OVER (), 0), 0)), 1) AS complexity_index,
         ROUND(100 * (0.35 * COALESCE(ln(1 + active_people) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + active_people)) OVER (), 0), 0) + 0.15 * COALESCE(ln(1 + active_service_principals) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + active_service_principals)) OVER (), 0), 0) + 0.3 * COALESCE(ln(1 + queries) / NULLIF(MAX(ln(1 + queries)) OVER (), 0), 0)) + 100 * 0.2 * LEAST(active_days, datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE))) / datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)), 1) AS usage_index
  FROM base
),
-- Every row of wd_workspaces.
b AS (
SELECT workspace, workspace_id,
       concat('https://', regexp_replace(regexp_replace(COALESCE(workspace_url, ''), '^https?://', ''), '/.*$', '')) AS workspace_link,
       tables_written_never_read, tables_idle, tables_cooling, tables_active,
       ROUND(data_bytes / 1073741824.0, 2) AS data_gb, ROUND(unread_bytes / 1073741824.0, 2) AS unread_gb,
       reads, writes, CAST(date_format(last_read, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_read,
       CAST(date_format(last_write, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_write, sensitive_tables,
       catalogs, tables, views, table_columns, view_columns, volumes, volume_files, complexity_index,
       active_people, active_service_principals, queries, active_days,
       CAST(date_format(last_query, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') AS STRING) AS last_query, usage_index,
       CASE WHEN usage_index < 10 THEN 'Retire candidate'
            WHEN complexity_index >= 50 AND usage_index < 50 THEN 'Review'
            WHEN complexity_index < 50 THEN 'Light'
            ELSE 'Healthy' END AS signal,
       CASE WHEN usage_index < 10 OR (complexity_index >= 50 AND usage_index < 50) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS flagged,
       1 AS workspaces
FROM idx
ORDER BY usage_index DESC, workspace
),
-- Ranked by read plus write events.
r AS (
  SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY reads + writes DESC, workspace) AS rnk FROM b WHERE reads + writes > 0
)
SELECT workspace, workspace AS name, 'Reads' AS direction, reads AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 50
UNION ALL
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT workspace, workspace AS name, 'Writes' AS direction, writes AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 50

                                  The most used tables

                                  Shows: The 15 with the most read and write events in the window, from wd_tables: one row per direction. Used by: The most active charts.

                                  Why it is built this way: The tables the platform runs on: break one of these and many people notice.

                                  How its tables join

                                  Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

                                    1. System tables it reads

                                    • system.access.table_lineage
                                    • system.access.workspaces_latest
                                    • system.information_schema.catalogs
                                    • system.information_schema.column_masks
                                    • system.information_schema.column_tags
                                    • system.information_schema.columns
                                    • system.information_schema.tables
                                    • system.storage.predictive_optimization_operations_history

                                    2. Steps (CTEs), in order

                                    1. wsl: Workspace id to name and URL.
                                      Its SQL (6 lines)
                                      -- The most used tables. Dates from the Time Window.
-- Workspace id to name and URL.
wsl AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name, workspace_url
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
                                    2. seen: Lineage reads and writes per catalog and workspace over 90 days.
                                      Its SQL (10 lines)
                                      -- Lineage reads and writes per catalog and workspace over 90 days.
seen AS (
  SELECT catalog, CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, COUNT(*) AS events FROM (
    SELECT workspace_id, source_table_catalog AS catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT workspace_id, target_table_catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  ) WHERE catalog IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1, 2
)
                                    3. home: Each catalog's home workspace: the one with the most of them.
                                      Its SQL (7 lines)
                                      -- Each catalog's home workspace: the one with the most of them.
home AS (
  SELECT c.catalog_name AS catalog, max_by(s.workspace_id, s.events) AS workspace_id
  FROM system.information_schema.catalogs c LEFT JOIN seen s ON s.catalog = c.catalog_name
  WHERE c.catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  GROUP BY c.catalog_name
)
                                    4. place: Each catalog with its home workspace's name and URL.
                                      Its SQL (7 lines)
                                      -- Each catalog with its home workspace's name and URL.
place AS (
  -- a catalog without lineage in 90 days keeps its rows, under No home workspace
  SELECT h.catalog, h.workspace_id, w.workspace_url,
         COALESCE(w.workspace_name, h.workspace_id, 'No home workspace') AS workspace
  FROM home h LEFT JOIN wsl w ON w.workspace_id = h.workspace_id
)
                                    5. t: Every table and view, without the system catalogs.
                                      Its SQL (7 lines)
                                      -- Every table and view, without the system catalogs.
t AS (
  SELECT table_catalog AS catalog, table_schema AS sch, table_name, table_type, table_owner, created,
         table_catalog || '.' || table_schema || '.' || table_name AS full_name
  FROM system.information_schema.tables
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
)
                                    6. lin: Every read (source) and write (target) of a table over 365 days, from lineage.
                                      Its SQL (9 lines)
                                      -- Every read (source) and write (target) of a table over 365 days, from lineage.
lin AS (
  SELECT source_table_full_name AS full_name, 1 AS is_read, 0 AS is_write, event_time, lower(created_by) AS who
  FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND source_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
  UNION ALL
  SELECT target_table_full_name, 0, 1, event_time, lower(created_by) FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND target_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
)
                                    7. used: Per table: reads, writes, readers and writers in the window; last read and write over 365 days.
                                      Its SQL (12 lines)
                                      -- Per table: reads, writes, readers and writers in the window; last read and write over 365 days.
used AS (
  -- counts in the Time Window; last read and write over the lineage's year
  SELECT full_name,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_read ELSE 0 END) AS reads,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_write ELSE 0 END) AS writes,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_read = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS readers,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_write = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS writers,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_read = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_read,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_write = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_write
  FROM lin GROUP BY 1
)
                                    8. hist: Per table, from the job's DESCRIBE HISTORY: last data change and its operation, last OPTIMIZE or VACUUM, rows changed and bytes written in the window.
                                      Its SQL (10 lines)
                                      -- Per table, from the job's DESCRIBE HISTORY: last data change and its operation, last OPTIMIZE or VACUUM, rows changed and bytes written in the window.
hist AS (
  SELECT catalog, schema, table_name,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change,
         max_by(operation, CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change_op,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'OPTIMIZE|VACUUM|ANALYZE|REORG' THEN timestamp END) AS last_maintenance,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN rows_changed ELSE 0 END) AS rows_changed,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN bytes_written ELSE 0 END) AS bytes_written
  FROM main.data_usage.table_history GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
)
                                    9. po: Per table: the last predictive optimization run.
                                      Its SQL (7 lines)
                                      -- Per table: the last predictive optimization run.
po AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS catalog, schema_name AS schema, table_name, MAX(start_time) AS last_po
  FROM system.storage.predictive_optimization_operations_history
  WHERE start_time >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365)
  GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
)
                                    10. found: Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag (pii, sensitive, gdpr, class.*), else the column name.
                                      Its SQL (21 lines)
                                      -- Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag (pii, sensitive, gdpr, class.*), else the column name.
found AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS c, schema_name AS s, table_name AS t, column_name,
         COALESCE(NULLIF(tag_value, ''), tag_name) AS pii_type, 1 AS rnk
  FROM system.information_schema.column_tags
  WHERE lower(tag_name) RLIKE '^(pii|sensitive|gdpr|class[.])' AND catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  UNION ALL
  SELECT table_catalog, table_schema, table_name, column_name,
         CASE
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'e_?mail' THEN 'Email'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'phone|mobile|msisdn' THEN 'Phone number'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'iban|(^|_)bic($|_)|account_?(no|number)' THEN 'IBAN / bank account'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'ssn|(^|_)ahv|passport|national_?id' THEN 'National ID'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE '(^|_)ip(_?address)?($|_)' THEN 'IP address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'address|street|(^|_)zip|postal' THEN 'Postal address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'birth|(^|_)dob($|_)' THEN 'Date of birth'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'card_?(no|number|num)|(^|_)pan$' THEN 'Payment card'
         END, 2
  FROM system.information_schema.columns
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
)
                                    11. sens_col: One row per sensitive column, the tag winning over the name, and whether a column mask covers it.
                                      Its SQL (9 lines)
                                      -- One row per sensitive column, the tag winning over the name, and whether a column mask covers it.
sens_col AS (
  SELECT f.c || '.' || f.s || '.' || f.t AS full_name, f.column_name, f.pii_type, cm.mask_name IS NOT NULL AS masked
  FROM found f
  LEFT JOIN system.information_schema.column_masks cm
    ON cm.table_catalog = f.c AND cm.table_schema = f.s AND cm.table_name = f.t AND cm.column_name = f.column_name
  WHERE f.pii_type IS NOT NULL
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY f.c, f.s, f.t, f.column_name ORDER BY f.rnk) = 1
)
                                    12. sens: Per table: sensitive columns, how many are unmasked, and their types.
                                      Its SQL (6 lines)
                                      -- Per table: sensitive columns, how many are unmasked, and their types.
sens AS (
  SELECT full_name, COUNT(*) AS sensitive_columns, COUNT_IF(NOT masked) AS unmasked_columns,
         concat_ws(', ', sort_array(collect_set(pii_type))) AS sensitive_types
  FROM sens_col GROUP BY 1
)
                                    13. j: Everything per table.
                                      Its SQL (19 lines)
                                      -- Everything per table.
j AS (
  SELECT p.workspace, p.workspace_id, p.workspace_url, t.catalog, t.sch AS schema_name, t.table_name AS name, t.full_name,
         t.table_type, t.table_owner, t.created,
         s.size_in_bytes, s.num_files, COALESCE(u.reads, 0) AS reads, COALESCE(u.writes, 0) AS writes,
         COALESCE(u.readers, 0) AS readers, COALESCE(u.writers, 0) AS writers, u.last_read,
         GREATEST(u.last_write, h.last_change) AS last_write, h.last_change_op,
         GREATEST(h.last_maintenance, po.last_po) AS last_maintenance,
         COALESCE(h.rows_changed, 0) AS rows_changed, COALESCE(h.bytes_written, 0) AS bytes_written,
         COALESCE(se.sensitive_columns, 0) AS sensitive_columns, COALESCE(se.unmasked_columns, 0) AS unmasked_columns,
         se.sensitive_types
  FROM t
  LEFT JOIN place p ON p.catalog = t.catalog
  LEFT JOIN main.data_usage.table_sizes s ON s.catalog = t.catalog AND s.schema = t.sch AND s.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN used u ON u.full_name = t.full_name
  LEFT JOIN hist h ON h.catalog = t.catalog AND h.schema = t.sch AND h.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN po ON po.catalog = t.catalog AND po.schema = t.sch AND po.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN sens se ON se.full_name = t.full_name
)
                                    14. c: Its usage class: Written, never read (writes, no read in the window), Idle 90d+ (no read or write in 90 days), Cooling (no read in the window), Active (read in the window, or created in the last 7 days).
                                      Its SQL (9 lines)
                                      -- Its usage class: Written, never read (writes, no read in the window), Idle 90d+ (no read or write in 90 days), Cooling (no read in the window), Active (read in the window, or created in the last 7 days).
c AS (
  SELECT *, CASE WHEN writes > 0 AND reads = 0 THEN 'Written, never read'
            WHEN reads = 0 AND writes = 0 AND last_read IS NULL AND last_write IS NULL
                 AND created >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 7) THEN 'Active'
            WHEN GREATEST(last_read, last_write) IS NULL OR datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(GREATEST(last_read, last_write) AS DATE)) >= 90 THEN 'Idle 90d+'
            WHEN reads = 0 THEN 'Cooling'
            ELSE 'Active' END AS usage_class FROM j
)
                                    15. b: Every row of wd_tables.
                                      Its SQL (25 lines)
                                      -- Every row of wd_tables.
b AS (
SELECT workspace, catalog, schema_name, catalog || '.' || schema_name AS schema_full, full_name AS table_name,
       CASE WHEN table_type = 'VIEW' THEN 'View' ELSE 'Table' END AS kind, usage_class, CASE usage_class WHEN 'Written, never read' THEN 1 WHEN 'Idle 90d+' THEN 2 WHEN 'Cooling' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END AS class_rank,
       size_in_bytes, ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) AS size_gb, num_files,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Written, never read' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_written_never_read,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Idle 90d+' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_idle,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Cooling' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_cooling,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Active' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_active,
       reads, writes, readers, writers,
       CAST(date_format(last_read, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_read,
       datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(last_read AS DATE)) AS days_since_read,
       CAST(date_format(last_write, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_write, lower(last_change_op) AS last_change_op,
       CAST(date_format(last_maintenance, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_maintenance,
       rows_changed, ROUND(bytes_written / 1073741824.0, 2) AS gb_written,
       sensitive_columns, unmasked_columns, COALESCE(sensitive_types, '') AS sensitive_types,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 AND usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive_unread,
       CASE WHEN unmasked_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS has_unmasked,
       CASE WHEN usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS unread_gb,
       CASE WHEN workspace_url IS NOT NULL THEN concat('https://', regexp_replace(regexp_replace(workspace_url, '^https?://', ''), '/.*$', ''), '/explore/data/', catalog, '/', schema_name, '/', name) END AS table_link,
       table_owner AS owner, CAST(date_format(created, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS created, 1 AS objects
FROM c
ORDER BY class_rank, size_in_bytes DESC NULLS LAST, table_name
)
                                    16. r: Ranked by read plus write events.
                                      Its SQL (6 lines)
                                      -- Ranked by read plus write events.
r AS (
  SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY reads + writes DESC, table_name) AS rnk FROM b WHERE reads + writes > 0
)
SELECT workspace, table_name AS name, 'Reads' AS direction, reads AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 15
UNION ALL

                                    3. Final query

                                    Up to 30 rows.

                                    4. Columns it returns

                                    ColumnMeaning
                                    workspaceThe workspace, by name: for a table or volume, the home workspace of its catalog.
                                    nameThe workspace, table, person or volume ranked.
                                    directionReads or Writes; on lineage, Upstream or Downstream.
                                    eventsLineage events.
                                    SQL with comments (155 lines)
                                    -- The most used tables. Dates from the Time Window.
-- Workspace id to name and URL.
WITH wsl AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name, workspace_url
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Lineage reads and writes per catalog and workspace over 90 days.
seen AS (
  SELECT catalog, CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, COUNT(*) AS events FROM (
    SELECT workspace_id, source_table_catalog AS catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT workspace_id, target_table_catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  ) WHERE catalog IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1, 2
),
-- Each catalog's home workspace: the one with the most of them.
home AS (
  SELECT c.catalog_name AS catalog, max_by(s.workspace_id, s.events) AS workspace_id
  FROM system.information_schema.catalogs c LEFT JOIN seen s ON s.catalog = c.catalog_name
  WHERE c.catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  GROUP BY c.catalog_name
),
-- Each catalog with its home workspace's name and URL.
place AS (
  -- a catalog without lineage in 90 days keeps its rows, under No home workspace
  SELECT h.catalog, h.workspace_id, w.workspace_url,
         COALESCE(w.workspace_name, h.workspace_id, 'No home workspace') AS workspace
  FROM home h LEFT JOIN wsl w ON w.workspace_id = h.workspace_id
),
-- Every table and view, without the system catalogs.
t AS (
  SELECT table_catalog AS catalog, table_schema AS sch, table_name, table_type, table_owner, created,
         table_catalog || '.' || table_schema || '.' || table_name AS full_name
  FROM system.information_schema.tables
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
),
-- Every read (source) and write (target) of a table over 365 days, from lineage.
lin AS (
  SELECT source_table_full_name AS full_name, 1 AS is_read, 0 AS is_write, event_time, lower(created_by) AS who
  FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND source_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
  UNION ALL
  SELECT target_table_full_name, 0, 1, event_time, lower(created_by) FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND target_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
),
-- Per table: reads, writes, readers and writers in the window; last read and write over 365 days.
used AS (
  -- counts in the Time Window; last read and write over the lineage's year
  SELECT full_name,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_read ELSE 0 END) AS reads,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_write ELSE 0 END) AS writes,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_read = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS readers,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_write = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS writers,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_read = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_read,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_write = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_write
  FROM lin GROUP BY 1
),
-- Per table, from the job's DESCRIBE HISTORY: last data change and its operation, last OPTIMIZE or VACUUM, rows changed and bytes written in the window.
hist AS (
  SELECT catalog, schema, table_name,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change,
         max_by(operation, CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change_op,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'OPTIMIZE|VACUUM|ANALYZE|REORG' THEN timestamp END) AS last_maintenance,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN rows_changed ELSE 0 END) AS rows_changed,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN bytes_written ELSE 0 END) AS bytes_written
  FROM main.data_usage.table_history GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
),
-- Per table: the last predictive optimization run.
po AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS catalog, schema_name AS schema, table_name, MAX(start_time) AS last_po
  FROM system.storage.predictive_optimization_operations_history
  WHERE start_time >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365)
  GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
),
-- Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag (pii, sensitive, gdpr, class.*), else the column name.
found AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS c, schema_name AS s, table_name AS t, column_name,
         COALESCE(NULLIF(tag_value, ''), tag_name) AS pii_type, 1 AS rnk
  FROM system.information_schema.column_tags
  WHERE lower(tag_name) RLIKE '^(pii|sensitive|gdpr|class[.])' AND catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  UNION ALL
  SELECT table_catalog, table_schema, table_name, column_name,
         CASE
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'e_?mail' THEN 'Email'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'phone|mobile|msisdn' THEN 'Phone number'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'iban|(^|_)bic($|_)|account_?(no|number)' THEN 'IBAN / bank account'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'ssn|(^|_)ahv|passport|national_?id' THEN 'National ID'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE '(^|_)ip(_?address)?($|_)' THEN 'IP address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'address|street|(^|_)zip|postal' THEN 'Postal address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'birth|(^|_)dob($|_)' THEN 'Date of birth'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'card_?(no|number|num)|(^|_)pan$' THEN 'Payment card'
         END, 2
  FROM system.information_schema.columns
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
),
-- One row per sensitive column, the tag winning over the name, and whether a column mask covers it.
sens_col AS (
  SELECT f.c || '.' || f.s || '.' || f.t AS full_name, f.column_name, f.pii_type, cm.mask_name IS NOT NULL AS masked
  FROM found f
  LEFT JOIN system.information_schema.column_masks cm
    ON cm.table_catalog = f.c AND cm.table_schema = f.s AND cm.table_name = f.t AND cm.column_name = f.column_name
  WHERE f.pii_type IS NOT NULL
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY f.c, f.s, f.t, f.column_name ORDER BY f.rnk) = 1
),
-- Per table: sensitive columns, how many are unmasked, and their types.
sens AS (
  SELECT full_name, COUNT(*) AS sensitive_columns, COUNT_IF(NOT masked) AS unmasked_columns,
         concat_ws(', ', sort_array(collect_set(pii_type))) AS sensitive_types
  FROM sens_col GROUP BY 1
),
-- Everything per table.
j AS (
  SELECT p.workspace, p.workspace_id, p.workspace_url, t.catalog, t.sch AS schema_name, t.table_name AS name, t.full_name,
         t.table_type, t.table_owner, t.created,
         s.size_in_bytes, s.num_files, COALESCE(u.reads, 0) AS reads, COALESCE(u.writes, 0) AS writes,
         COALESCE(u.readers, 0) AS readers, COALESCE(u.writers, 0) AS writers, u.last_read,
         GREATEST(u.last_write, h.last_change) AS last_write, h.last_change_op,
         GREATEST(h.last_maintenance, po.last_po) AS last_maintenance,
         COALESCE(h.rows_changed, 0) AS rows_changed, COALESCE(h.bytes_written, 0) AS bytes_written,
         COALESCE(se.sensitive_columns, 0) AS sensitive_columns, COALESCE(se.unmasked_columns, 0) AS unmasked_columns,
         se.sensitive_types
  FROM t
  LEFT JOIN place p ON p.catalog = t.catalog
  LEFT JOIN main.data_usage.table_sizes s ON s.catalog = t.catalog AND s.schema = t.sch AND s.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN used u ON u.full_name = t.full_name
  LEFT JOIN hist h ON h.catalog = t.catalog AND h.schema = t.sch AND h.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN po ON po.catalog = t.catalog AND po.schema = t.sch AND po.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN sens se ON se.full_name = t.full_name
),
-- Its usage class: Written, never read (writes, no read in the window), Idle 90d+ (no read or write in 90 days), Cooling (no read in the window), Active (read in the window, or created in the last 7 days).
c AS (
  SELECT *, CASE WHEN writes > 0 AND reads = 0 THEN 'Written, never read'
            WHEN reads = 0 AND writes = 0 AND last_read IS NULL AND last_write IS NULL
                 AND created >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 7) THEN 'Active'
            WHEN GREATEST(last_read, last_write) IS NULL OR datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(GREATEST(last_read, last_write) AS DATE)) >= 90 THEN 'Idle 90d+'
            WHEN reads = 0 THEN 'Cooling'
            ELSE 'Active' END AS usage_class FROM j
),
-- Every row of wd_tables.
b AS (
SELECT workspace, catalog, schema_name, catalog || '.' || schema_name AS schema_full, full_name AS table_name,
       CASE WHEN table_type = 'VIEW' THEN 'View' ELSE 'Table' END AS kind, usage_class, CASE usage_class WHEN 'Written, never read' THEN 1 WHEN 'Idle 90d+' THEN 2 WHEN 'Cooling' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END AS class_rank,
       size_in_bytes, ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) AS size_gb, num_files,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Written, never read' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_written_never_read,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Idle 90d+' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_idle,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Cooling' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_cooling,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Active' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_active,
       reads, writes, readers, writers,
       CAST(date_format(last_read, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_read,
       datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(last_read AS DATE)) AS days_since_read,
       CAST(date_format(last_write, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_write, lower(last_change_op) AS last_change_op,
       CAST(date_format(last_maintenance, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_maintenance,
       rows_changed, ROUND(bytes_written / 1073741824.0, 2) AS gb_written,
       sensitive_columns, unmasked_columns, COALESCE(sensitive_types, '') AS sensitive_types,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 AND usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive_unread,
       CASE WHEN unmasked_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS has_unmasked,
       CASE WHEN usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS unread_gb,
       CASE WHEN workspace_url IS NOT NULL THEN concat('https://', regexp_replace(regexp_replace(workspace_url, '^https?://', ''), '/.*$', ''), '/explore/data/', catalog, '/', schema_name, '/', name) END AS table_link,
       table_owner AS owner, CAST(date_format(created, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS created, 1 AS objects
FROM c
ORDER BY class_rank, size_in_bytes DESC NULLS LAST, table_name
),
-- Ranked by read plus write events.
r AS (
  SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY reads + writes DESC, table_name) AS rnk FROM b WHERE reads + writes > 0
)
SELECT workspace, table_name AS name, 'Reads' AS direction, reads AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 15
UNION ALL
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT workspace, table_name AS name, 'Writes' AS direction, writes AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 15

                                    Each workspace's most used tables

                                    Shows: The 10 with the most read and write events in the window in each workspace, from wd_tables: one row per direction. Used by: The most active charts.

                                    Why it is built this way: The same inside one workspace: what its owners should protect first.

                                    How its tables join

                                    Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

                                      1. System tables it reads

                                      • system.access.table_lineage
                                      • system.access.workspaces_latest
                                      • system.information_schema.catalogs
                                      • system.information_schema.column_masks
                                      • system.information_schema.column_tags
                                      • system.information_schema.columns
                                      • system.information_schema.tables
                                      • system.storage.predictive_optimization_operations_history

                                      2. Steps (CTEs), in order

                                      1. wsl: Workspace id to name and URL.
                                        Its SQL (6 lines)
                                        -- Each workspace's most used tables. Dates from the Time Window.
-- Workspace id to name and URL.
wsl AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name, workspace_url
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
                                      2. seen: Lineage reads and writes per catalog and workspace over 90 days.
                                        Its SQL (10 lines)
                                        -- Lineage reads and writes per catalog and workspace over 90 days.
seen AS (
  SELECT catalog, CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, COUNT(*) AS events FROM (
    SELECT workspace_id, source_table_catalog AS catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT workspace_id, target_table_catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  ) WHERE catalog IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1, 2
)
                                      3. home: Each catalog's home workspace: the one with the most of them.
                                        Its SQL (7 lines)
                                        -- Each catalog's home workspace: the one with the most of them.
home AS (
  SELECT c.catalog_name AS catalog, max_by(s.workspace_id, s.events) AS workspace_id
  FROM system.information_schema.catalogs c LEFT JOIN seen s ON s.catalog = c.catalog_name
  WHERE c.catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  GROUP BY c.catalog_name
)
                                      4. place: Each catalog with its home workspace's name and URL.
                                        Its SQL (7 lines)
                                        -- Each catalog with its home workspace's name and URL.
place AS (
  -- a catalog without lineage in 90 days keeps its rows, under No home workspace
  SELECT h.catalog, h.workspace_id, w.workspace_url,
         COALESCE(w.workspace_name, h.workspace_id, 'No home workspace') AS workspace
  FROM home h LEFT JOIN wsl w ON w.workspace_id = h.workspace_id
)
                                      5. t: Every table and view, without the system catalogs.
                                        Its SQL (7 lines)
                                        -- Every table and view, without the system catalogs.
t AS (
  SELECT table_catalog AS catalog, table_schema AS sch, table_name, table_type, table_owner, created,
         table_catalog || '.' || table_schema || '.' || table_name AS full_name
  FROM system.information_schema.tables
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
)
                                      6. lin: Every read (source) and write (target) of a table over 365 days, from lineage.
                                        Its SQL (9 lines)
                                        -- Every read (source) and write (target) of a table over 365 days, from lineage.
lin AS (
  SELECT source_table_full_name AS full_name, 1 AS is_read, 0 AS is_write, event_time, lower(created_by) AS who
  FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND source_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
  UNION ALL
  SELECT target_table_full_name, 0, 1, event_time, lower(created_by) FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND target_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
)
                                      7. used: Per table: reads, writes, readers and writers in the window; last read and write over 365 days.
                                        Its SQL (12 lines)
                                        -- Per table: reads, writes, readers and writers in the window; last read and write over 365 days.
used AS (
  -- counts in the Time Window; last read and write over the lineage's year
  SELECT full_name,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_read ELSE 0 END) AS reads,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_write ELSE 0 END) AS writes,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_read = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS readers,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_write = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS writers,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_read = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_read,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_write = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_write
  FROM lin GROUP BY 1
)
                                      8. hist: Per table, from the job's DESCRIBE HISTORY: last data change and its operation, last OPTIMIZE or VACUUM, rows changed and bytes written in the window.
                                        Its SQL (10 lines)
                                        -- Per table, from the job's DESCRIBE HISTORY: last data change and its operation, last OPTIMIZE or VACUUM, rows changed and bytes written in the window.
hist AS (
  SELECT catalog, schema, table_name,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change,
         max_by(operation, CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change_op,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'OPTIMIZE|VACUUM|ANALYZE|REORG' THEN timestamp END) AS last_maintenance,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN rows_changed ELSE 0 END) AS rows_changed,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN bytes_written ELSE 0 END) AS bytes_written
  FROM main.data_usage.table_history GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
)
                                      9. po: Per table: the last predictive optimization run.
                                        Its SQL (7 lines)
                                        -- Per table: the last predictive optimization run.
po AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS catalog, schema_name AS schema, table_name, MAX(start_time) AS last_po
  FROM system.storage.predictive_optimization_operations_history
  WHERE start_time >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365)
  GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
)
                                      10. found: Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag (pii, sensitive, gdpr, class.*), else the column name.
                                        Its SQL (21 lines)
                                        -- Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag (pii, sensitive, gdpr, class.*), else the column name.
found AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS c, schema_name AS s, table_name AS t, column_name,
         COALESCE(NULLIF(tag_value, ''), tag_name) AS pii_type, 1 AS rnk
  FROM system.information_schema.column_tags
  WHERE lower(tag_name) RLIKE '^(pii|sensitive|gdpr|class[.])' AND catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  UNION ALL
  SELECT table_catalog, table_schema, table_name, column_name,
         CASE
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'e_?mail' THEN 'Email'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'phone|mobile|msisdn' THEN 'Phone number'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'iban|(^|_)bic($|_)|account_?(no|number)' THEN 'IBAN / bank account'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'ssn|(^|_)ahv|passport|national_?id' THEN 'National ID'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE '(^|_)ip(_?address)?($|_)' THEN 'IP address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'address|street|(^|_)zip|postal' THEN 'Postal address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'birth|(^|_)dob($|_)' THEN 'Date of birth'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'card_?(no|number|num)|(^|_)pan$' THEN 'Payment card'
         END, 2
  FROM system.information_schema.columns
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
)
                                      11. sens_col: One row per sensitive column, the tag winning over the name, and whether a column mask covers it.
                                        Its SQL (9 lines)
                                        -- One row per sensitive column, the tag winning over the name, and whether a column mask covers it.
sens_col AS (
  SELECT f.c || '.' || f.s || '.' || f.t AS full_name, f.column_name, f.pii_type, cm.mask_name IS NOT NULL AS masked
  FROM found f
  LEFT JOIN system.information_schema.column_masks cm
    ON cm.table_catalog = f.c AND cm.table_schema = f.s AND cm.table_name = f.t AND cm.column_name = f.column_name
  WHERE f.pii_type IS NOT NULL
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY f.c, f.s, f.t, f.column_name ORDER BY f.rnk) = 1
)
                                      12. sens: Per table: sensitive columns, how many are unmasked, and their types.
                                        Its SQL (6 lines)
                                        -- Per table: sensitive columns, how many are unmasked, and their types.
sens AS (
  SELECT full_name, COUNT(*) AS sensitive_columns, COUNT_IF(NOT masked) AS unmasked_columns,
         concat_ws(', ', sort_array(collect_set(pii_type))) AS sensitive_types
  FROM sens_col GROUP BY 1
)
                                      13. j: Everything per table.
                                        Its SQL (19 lines)
                                        -- Everything per table.
j AS (
  SELECT p.workspace, p.workspace_id, p.workspace_url, t.catalog, t.sch AS schema_name, t.table_name AS name, t.full_name,
         t.table_type, t.table_owner, t.created,
         s.size_in_bytes, s.num_files, COALESCE(u.reads, 0) AS reads, COALESCE(u.writes, 0) AS writes,
         COALESCE(u.readers, 0) AS readers, COALESCE(u.writers, 0) AS writers, u.last_read,
         GREATEST(u.last_write, h.last_change) AS last_write, h.last_change_op,
         GREATEST(h.last_maintenance, po.last_po) AS last_maintenance,
         COALESCE(h.rows_changed, 0) AS rows_changed, COALESCE(h.bytes_written, 0) AS bytes_written,
         COALESCE(se.sensitive_columns, 0) AS sensitive_columns, COALESCE(se.unmasked_columns, 0) AS unmasked_columns,
         se.sensitive_types
  FROM t
  LEFT JOIN place p ON p.catalog = t.catalog
  LEFT JOIN main.data_usage.table_sizes s ON s.catalog = t.catalog AND s.schema = t.sch AND s.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN used u ON u.full_name = t.full_name
  LEFT JOIN hist h ON h.catalog = t.catalog AND h.schema = t.sch AND h.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN po ON po.catalog = t.catalog AND po.schema = t.sch AND po.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN sens se ON se.full_name = t.full_name
)
                                      14. c: Its usage class: Written, never read (writes, no read in the window), Idle 90d+ (no read or write in 90 days), Cooling (no read in the window), Active (read in the window, or created in the last 7 days).
                                        Its SQL (9 lines)
                                        -- Its usage class: Written, never read (writes, no read in the window), Idle 90d+ (no read or write in 90 days), Cooling (no read in the window), Active (read in the window, or created in the last 7 days).
c AS (
  SELECT *, CASE WHEN writes > 0 AND reads = 0 THEN 'Written, never read'
            WHEN reads = 0 AND writes = 0 AND last_read IS NULL AND last_write IS NULL
                 AND created >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 7) THEN 'Active'
            WHEN GREATEST(last_read, last_write) IS NULL OR datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(GREATEST(last_read, last_write) AS DATE)) >= 90 THEN 'Idle 90d+'
            WHEN reads = 0 THEN 'Cooling'
            ELSE 'Active' END AS usage_class FROM j
)
                                      15. b: Every row of wd_tables.
                                        Its SQL (25 lines)
                                        -- Every row of wd_tables.
b AS (
SELECT workspace, catalog, schema_name, catalog || '.' || schema_name AS schema_full, full_name AS table_name,
       CASE WHEN table_type = 'VIEW' THEN 'View' ELSE 'Table' END AS kind, usage_class, CASE usage_class WHEN 'Written, never read' THEN 1 WHEN 'Idle 90d+' THEN 2 WHEN 'Cooling' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END AS class_rank,
       size_in_bytes, ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) AS size_gb, num_files,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Written, never read' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_written_never_read,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Idle 90d+' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_idle,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Cooling' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_cooling,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Active' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_active,
       reads, writes, readers, writers,
       CAST(date_format(last_read, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_read,
       datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(last_read AS DATE)) AS days_since_read,
       CAST(date_format(last_write, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_write, lower(last_change_op) AS last_change_op,
       CAST(date_format(last_maintenance, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_maintenance,
       rows_changed, ROUND(bytes_written / 1073741824.0, 2) AS gb_written,
       sensitive_columns, unmasked_columns, COALESCE(sensitive_types, '') AS sensitive_types,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 AND usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive_unread,
       CASE WHEN unmasked_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS has_unmasked,
       CASE WHEN usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS unread_gb,
       CASE WHEN workspace_url IS NOT NULL THEN concat('https://', regexp_replace(regexp_replace(workspace_url, '^https?://', ''), '/.*$', ''), '/explore/data/', catalog, '/', schema_name, '/', name) END AS table_link,
       table_owner AS owner, CAST(date_format(created, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS created, 1 AS objects
FROM c
ORDER BY class_rank, size_in_bytes DESC NULLS LAST, table_name
)
                                      16. r: Ranked by read plus write events, within each workspace.
                                        Its SQL (6 lines)
                                        -- Ranked by read plus write events, within each workspace.
r AS (
  SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace ORDER BY reads + writes DESC, table_name) AS rnk FROM b WHERE reads + writes > 0
)
SELECT workspace, table_name AS name, 'Reads' AS direction, reads AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 10
UNION ALL

                                      3. Final query

                                      Up to 20 rows per workspace.

                                      4. Columns it returns

                                      ColumnMeaning
                                      workspaceThe workspace, by name: for a table or volume, the home workspace of its catalog.
                                      nameThe workspace, table, person or volume ranked.
                                      directionReads or Writes; on lineage, Upstream or Downstream.
                                      eventsLineage events.
                                      SQL with comments (155 lines)
                                      -- Each workspace's most used tables. Dates from the Time Window.
-- Workspace id to name and URL.
WITH wsl AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name, workspace_url
  FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
),
-- Lineage reads and writes per catalog and workspace over 90 days.
seen AS (
  SELECT catalog, CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, COUNT(*) AS events FROM (
    SELECT workspace_id, source_table_catalog AS catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT workspace_id, target_table_catalog FROM system.access.table_lineage
    WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 90) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  ) WHERE catalog IS NOT NULL GROUP BY 1, 2
),
-- Each catalog's home workspace: the one with the most of them.
home AS (
  SELECT c.catalog_name AS catalog, max_by(s.workspace_id, s.events) AS workspace_id
  FROM system.information_schema.catalogs c LEFT JOIN seen s ON s.catalog = c.catalog_name
  WHERE c.catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  GROUP BY c.catalog_name
),
-- Each catalog with its home workspace's name and URL.
place AS (
  -- a catalog without lineage in 90 days keeps its rows, under No home workspace
  SELECT h.catalog, h.workspace_id, w.workspace_url,
         COALESCE(w.workspace_name, h.workspace_id, 'No home workspace') AS workspace
  FROM home h LEFT JOIN wsl w ON w.workspace_id = h.workspace_id
),
-- Every table and view, without the system catalogs.
t AS (
  SELECT table_catalog AS catalog, table_schema AS sch, table_name, table_type, table_owner, created,
         table_catalog || '.' || table_schema || '.' || table_name AS full_name
  FROM system.information_schema.tables
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
),
-- Every read (source) and write (target) of a table over 365 days, from lineage.
lin AS (
  SELECT source_table_full_name AS full_name, 1 AS is_read, 0 AS is_write, event_time, lower(created_by) AS who
  FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND source_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
  UNION ALL
  SELECT target_table_full_name, 0, 1, event_time, lower(created_by) FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND target_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
),
-- Per table: reads, writes, readers and writers in the window; last read and write over 365 days.
used AS (
  -- counts in the Time Window; last read and write over the lineage's year
  SELECT full_name,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_read ELSE 0 END) AS reads,
         SUM(CASE WHEN event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN is_write ELSE 0 END) AS writes,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_read = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS readers,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN is_write = 1 AND event_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN who END) AS writers,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_read = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_read,
         MAX(CASE WHEN is_write = 1 THEN event_time END) AS last_write
  FROM lin GROUP BY 1
),
-- Per table, from the job's DESCRIBE HISTORY: last data change and its operation, last OPTIMIZE or VACUUM, rows changed and bytes written in the window.
hist AS (
  SELECT catalog, schema, table_name,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change,
         max_by(operation, CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'WRITE|MERGE|UPDATE|DELETE|STREAMING UPDATE|CREATE OR REPLACE|COPY INTO|TRUNCATE|RESTORE' THEN timestamp END) AS last_change_op,
         MAX(CASE WHEN operation RLIKE 'OPTIMIZE|VACUUM|ANALYZE|REORG' THEN timestamp END) AS last_maintenance,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN rows_changed ELSE 0 END) AS rows_changed,
         SUM(CASE WHEN timestamp >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN bytes_written ELSE 0 END) AS bytes_written
  FROM main.data_usage.table_history GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
),
-- Per table: the last predictive optimization run.
po AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS catalog, schema_name AS schema, table_name, MAX(start_time) AS last_po
  FROM system.storage.predictive_optimization_operations_history
  WHERE start_time >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365)
  GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
),
-- Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag (pii, sensitive, gdpr, class.*), else the column name.
found AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS c, schema_name AS s, table_name AS t, column_name,
         COALESCE(NULLIF(tag_value, ''), tag_name) AS pii_type, 1 AS rnk
  FROM system.information_schema.column_tags
  WHERE lower(tag_name) RLIKE '^(pii|sensitive|gdpr|class[.])' AND catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  UNION ALL
  SELECT table_catalog, table_schema, table_name, column_name,
         CASE
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'e_?mail' THEN 'Email'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'phone|mobile|msisdn' THEN 'Phone number'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'iban|(^|_)bic($|_)|account_?(no|number)' THEN 'IBAN / bank account'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'ssn|(^|_)ahv|passport|national_?id' THEN 'National ID'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE '(^|_)ip(_?address)?($|_)' THEN 'IP address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'address|street|(^|_)zip|postal' THEN 'Postal address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'birth|(^|_)dob($|_)' THEN 'Date of birth'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'card_?(no|number|num)|(^|_)pan$' THEN 'Payment card'
         END, 2
  FROM system.information_schema.columns
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
),
-- One row per sensitive column, the tag winning over the name, and whether a column mask covers it.
sens_col AS (
  SELECT f.c || '.' || f.s || '.' || f.t AS full_name, f.column_name, f.pii_type, cm.mask_name IS NOT NULL AS masked
  FROM found f
  LEFT JOIN system.information_schema.column_masks cm
    ON cm.table_catalog = f.c AND cm.table_schema = f.s AND cm.table_name = f.t AND cm.column_name = f.column_name
  WHERE f.pii_type IS NOT NULL
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY f.c, f.s, f.t, f.column_name ORDER BY f.rnk) = 1
),
-- Per table: sensitive columns, how many are unmasked, and their types.
sens AS (
  SELECT full_name, COUNT(*) AS sensitive_columns, COUNT_IF(NOT masked) AS unmasked_columns,
         concat_ws(', ', sort_array(collect_set(pii_type))) AS sensitive_types
  FROM sens_col GROUP BY 1
),
-- Everything per table.
j AS (
  SELECT p.workspace, p.workspace_id, p.workspace_url, t.catalog, t.sch AS schema_name, t.table_name AS name, t.full_name,
         t.table_type, t.table_owner, t.created,
         s.size_in_bytes, s.num_files, COALESCE(u.reads, 0) AS reads, COALESCE(u.writes, 0) AS writes,
         COALESCE(u.readers, 0) AS readers, COALESCE(u.writers, 0) AS writers, u.last_read,
         GREATEST(u.last_write, h.last_change) AS last_write, h.last_change_op,
         GREATEST(h.last_maintenance, po.last_po) AS last_maintenance,
         COALESCE(h.rows_changed, 0) AS rows_changed, COALESCE(h.bytes_written, 0) AS bytes_written,
         COALESCE(se.sensitive_columns, 0) AS sensitive_columns, COALESCE(se.unmasked_columns, 0) AS unmasked_columns,
         se.sensitive_types
  FROM t
  LEFT JOIN place p ON p.catalog = t.catalog
  LEFT JOIN main.data_usage.table_sizes s ON s.catalog = t.catalog AND s.schema = t.sch AND s.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN used u ON u.full_name = t.full_name
  LEFT JOIN hist h ON h.catalog = t.catalog AND h.schema = t.sch AND h.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN po ON po.catalog = t.catalog AND po.schema = t.sch AND po.table_name = t.table_name
  LEFT JOIN sens se ON se.full_name = t.full_name
),
-- Its usage class: Written, never read (writes, no read in the window), Idle 90d+ (no read or write in 90 days), Cooling (no read in the window), Active (read in the window, or created in the last 7 days).
c AS (
  SELECT *, CASE WHEN writes > 0 AND reads = 0 THEN 'Written, never read'
            WHEN reads = 0 AND writes = 0 AND last_read IS NULL AND last_write IS NULL
                 AND created >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 7) THEN 'Active'
            WHEN GREATEST(last_read, last_write) IS NULL OR datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(GREATEST(last_read, last_write) AS DATE)) >= 90 THEN 'Idle 90d+'
            WHEN reads = 0 THEN 'Cooling'
            ELSE 'Active' END AS usage_class FROM j
),
-- Every row of wd_tables.
b AS (
SELECT workspace, catalog, schema_name, catalog || '.' || schema_name AS schema_full, full_name AS table_name,
       CASE WHEN table_type = 'VIEW' THEN 'View' ELSE 'Table' END AS kind, usage_class, CASE usage_class WHEN 'Written, never read' THEN 1 WHEN 'Idle 90d+' THEN 2 WHEN 'Cooling' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END AS class_rank,
       size_in_bytes, ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) AS size_gb, num_files,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Written, never read' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_written_never_read,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Idle 90d+' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_idle,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Cooling' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_cooling,
       CASE WHEN usage_class = 'Active' THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS gb_active,
       reads, writes, readers, writers,
       CAST(date_format(last_read, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_read,
       datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(last_read AS DATE)) AS days_since_read,
       CAST(date_format(last_write, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_write, lower(last_change_op) AS last_change_op,
       CAST(date_format(last_maintenance, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_maintenance,
       rows_changed, ROUND(bytes_written / 1073741824.0, 2) AS gb_written,
       sensitive_columns, unmasked_columns, COALESCE(sensitive_types, '') AS sensitive_types,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive,
       CASE WHEN sensitive_columns > 0 AND usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS sensitive_unread,
       CASE WHEN unmasked_columns > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS has_unmasked,
       CASE WHEN usage_class IN ('Written, never read', 'Idle 90d+') THEN ROUND(size_in_bytes / 1073741824.0, 3) ELSE 0 END AS unread_gb,
       CASE WHEN workspace_url IS NOT NULL THEN concat('https://', regexp_replace(regexp_replace(workspace_url, '^https?://', ''), '/.*$', ''), '/explore/data/', catalog, '/', schema_name, '/', name) END AS table_link,
       table_owner AS owner, CAST(date_format(created, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS created, 1 AS objects
FROM c
ORDER BY class_rank, size_in_bytes DESC NULLS LAST, table_name
),
-- Ranked by read plus write events, within each workspace.
r AS (
  SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY workspace ORDER BY reads + writes DESC, table_name) AS rnk FROM b WHERE reads + writes > 0
)
SELECT workspace, table_name AS name, 'Reads' AS direction, reads AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 10
UNION ALL
-- Final SELECT: the rows the widgets read.
SELECT workspace, table_name AS name, 'Writes' AS direction, writes AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 10

                                      The most active people

                                      Shows: The 15 with the most read and write events in the window, from wd_people: one row per direction. Used by: The most active charts.

                                      Why it is built this way: Who reads and writes most: the people to ask before changing shared data.

                                      How its tables join

                                      Grey: system tables. Blue border: a shared check (its steps are listed under Steps). White: steps of the query. Green: the rows the dashboard shows. ◆ marks the columns the joins use.

                                        1. System tables it reads

                                        • system.access.audit
                                        • system.access.table_lineage
                                        • system.access.workspaces_latest
                                        • system.information_schema.column_masks
                                        • system.information_schema.column_tags
                                        • system.information_schema.columns
                                        • system.information_schema.table_privileges
                                        • system.query.history

                                        2. Steps (CTEs), in order

                                        1. wsl: Workspace id to name.
                                          Its SQL (5 lines)
                                          -- The most active people. Dates from the Time Window; last seen over a year.
-- Workspace id to name.
wsl AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, workspace_name FROM system.access.workspaces_latest
)
                                        2. aev: Audit events over the last year, per workspace and identity, with successful logins marked.
                                          Its SQL (8 lines)
                                          -- Audit events over the last year, per workspace and identity, with successful logins marked.
aev AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, lower(COALESCE(user_identity.email, user_identity.subject_name)) AS who,
         event_date, event_time,
         service_name = 'accounts' AND action_name RLIKE '(?i)login' AND response.status_code = 200 AS is_login
  FROM system.access.audit
  WHERE event_date >= date_sub(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), 365) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
)
                                        3. seen: Per workspace and identity: last seen over the year, logins in the window.
                                          Its SQL (7 lines)
                                          -- Per workspace and identity: last seen over the year, logins in the window.
seen AS (
  -- last seen over the year; logins in the window
  SELECT workspace_id, who, MAX(event_time) AS last_seen, COUNT_IF(is_login AND event_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE)) AS logins,
         MAX(CASE WHEN event_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS seen_in_window
  FROM aev GROUP BY 1, 2
)
                                        4. qh: Queries per workspace and identity in the window.
                                          Its SQL (6 lines)
                                          -- Queries per workspace and identity in the window.
qh AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, lower(executed_by) AS who, COUNT(*) AS queries, MAX(start_time) AS last_query
  FROM system.query.history WHERE start_time >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) AND start_time < date_add(:time_range.max, 1)
  GROUP BY 1, 2
)
                                        5. lin: Table reads (source) and writes (target) in the window, per workspace and identity, from lineage.
                                          Its SQL (11 lines)
                                          -- Table reads (source) and writes (target) in the window, per workspace and identity, from lineage.
lin AS (
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING) AS workspace_id, source_table_full_name AS full_name, 1 AS is_read, 0 AS is_write,
         lower(created_by) AS who
  FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND source_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
  UNION ALL
  SELECT CAST(workspace_id AS STRING), target_table_full_name, 0, 1, lower(created_by)
  FROM system.access.table_lineage
  WHERE event_date >= CAST(:time_range.min AS DATE) AND event_date < date_add(:time_range.max, 1) AND target_table_full_name IS NOT NULL
)
                                        6. read_any: Tables anyone read in the window.
                                          Its SQL (2 lines)
                                          -- Tables anyone read in the window.
read_any AS (SELECT DISTINCT full_name FROM lin WHERE is_read = 1)
                                        7. found: Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag, else the column name.
                                          Its SQL (21 lines)
                                          -- Sensitive-looking columns: a column tag, else the column name.
found AS (
  SELECT catalog_name AS c, schema_name AS s, table_name AS t, column_name,
         COALESCE(NULLIF(tag_value, ''), tag_name) AS pii_type, 1 AS rnk
  FROM system.information_schema.column_tags
  WHERE lower(tag_name) RLIKE '^(pii|sensitive|gdpr|class[.])' AND catalog_name NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  UNION ALL
  SELECT table_catalog, table_schema, table_name, column_name,
         CASE
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'e_?mail' THEN 'Email'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'phone|mobile|msisdn' THEN 'Phone number'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'iban|(^|_)bic($|_)|account_?(no|number)' THEN 'IBAN / bank account'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'ssn|(^|_)ahv|passport|national_?id' THEN 'National ID'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE '(^|_)ip(_?address)?($|_)' THEN 'IP address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'address|street|(^|_)zip|postal' THEN 'Postal address'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'birth|(^|_)dob($|_)' THEN 'Date of birth'
         WHEN lower(column_name) RLIKE 'card_?(no|number|num)|(^|_)pan$' THEN 'Payment card'
         END, 2
  FROM system.information_schema.columns
  WHERE table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal') AND table_schema <> 'information_schema'
)
                                        8. sens_col: One row per sensitive column, and whether a mask covers it.
                                          Its SQL (9 lines)
                                          -- One row per sensitive column, and whether a mask covers it.
sens_col AS (
  SELECT f.c || '.' || f.s || '.' || f.t AS full_name, f.column_name, f.pii_type, cm.mask_name IS NOT NULL AS masked
  FROM found f
  LEFT JOIN system.information_schema.column_masks cm
    ON cm.table_catalog = f.c AND cm.table_schema = f.s AND cm.table_name = f.t AND cm.column_name = f.column_name
  WHERE f.pii_type IS NOT NULL
  QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY f.c, f.s, f.t, f.column_name ORDER BY f.rnk) = 1
)
                                        9. sens: Per table: its sensitive columns.
                                          Its SQL (6 lines)
                                          -- Per table: its sensitive columns.
sens AS (
  SELECT full_name, COUNT(*) AS sensitive_columns, COUNT_IF(NOT masked) AS unmasked_columns,
         concat_ws(', ', sort_array(collect_set(pii_type))) AS sensitive_types
  FROM sens_col GROUP BY 1
)
                                        10. tw: Per workspace and identity: tables read and written, writes nobody reads, sensitive tables touched.
                                          Its SQL (13 lines)
                                          -- Per workspace and identity: tables read and written, writes nobody reads, sensitive tables touched.
tw AS (
  SELECT l.workspace_id, l.who,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN l.is_read = 1 THEN l.full_name END) AS tables_read,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN l.is_write = 1 THEN l.full_name END) AS tables_written,
         SUM(l.is_read) AS reads, SUM(l.is_write) AS writes,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN l.is_write = 1 AND r.full_name IS NULL THEN l.full_name END) AS writes_nobody_reads,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN se.full_name IS NOT NULL THEN l.full_name END) AS sensitive_tables
  FROM lin l
  LEFT JOIN read_any r ON r.full_name = l.full_name
  LEFT JOIN sens se ON se.full_name = l.full_name
  GROUP BY 1, 2
)
                                        11. grants: Per grantee: tables it can read through a direct SELECT or ALL PRIVILEGES grant, and how many are sensitive.
                                          Its SQL (10 lines)
                                          -- Per grantee: tables it can read through a direct SELECT or ALL PRIVILEGES grant, and how many are sensitive.
grants AS (
  -- direct grants on tables, per grantee; through a schema, catalog or group isn't expanded
  SELECT lower(p.grantee) AS who, COUNT(DISTINCT p.table_catalog || '.' || p.table_schema || '.' || p.table_name) AS direct_grants,
         COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN se.full_name IS NOT NULL THEN p.table_catalog || '.' || p.table_schema || '.' || p.table_name END) AS sensitive_grants
  FROM system.information_schema.table_privileges p
  LEFT JOIN sens se ON se.full_name = p.table_catalog || '.' || p.table_schema || '.' || p.table_name
  WHERE p.privilege_type IN ('SELECT', 'ALL PRIVILEGES') AND p.table_catalog NOT IN ('system', 'samples', '__databricks_internal')
  GROUP BY 1
)
                                        12. ids: Every workspace and identity seen in any of them.
                                          Its SQL (4 lines)
                                          -- Every workspace and identity seen in any of them.
ids AS (
  SELECT workspace_id, who FROM seen UNION SELECT workspace_id, who FROM qh UNION SELECT workspace_id, who FROM tw
)
                                        13. k: All of it together, with the kind: a person (email) or a service principal (UUID).
                                          Its SQL (16 lines)
                                          -- All of it together, with the kind: a person (email) or a service principal (UUID).
k AS (
  SELECT i.workspace_id, i.who, CASE WHEN i.who RLIKE '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' THEN 'Service principal' WHEN i.who LIKE '%@%' THEN 'Person' ELSE 'Other' END AS kind,
         COALESCE(s.logins, 0) AS logins, COALESCE(q.queries, 0) AS queries,
         COALESCE(w.tables_read, 0) AS tables_read, COALESCE(w.tables_written, 0) AS tables_written,
         COALESCE(w.reads, 0) AS reads, COALESCE(w.writes, 0) AS writes,
         COALESCE(w.writes_nobody_reads, 0) AS writes_nobody_reads, COALESCE(w.sensitive_tables, 0) AS sensitive_tables,
         COALESCE(g.direct_grants, 0) AS direct_grants, COALESCE(g.sensitive_grants, 0) AS sensitive_grants,
         GREATEST(s.last_seen, q.last_query) AS last_seen,
         COALESCE(s.seen_in_window, 0) + CASE WHEN q.queries > 0 OR w.reads + w.writes > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS seen_in_window
  FROM ids i
  LEFT JOIN seen s ON s.workspace_id = i.workspace_id AND s.who = i.who
  LEFT JOIN qh q ON q.workspace_id = i.workspace_id AND q.who = i.who
  LEFT JOIN tw w ON w.workspace_id = i.workspace_id AND w.who = i.who
  LEFT JOIN grants g ON g.who = i.who
)
                                        14. people: Per row: last seen band, inactive when not seen for 90+ days, active when seen in the window.
                                          Its SQL (21 lines)
                                          -- Per row: last seen band, inactive when not seen for 90+ days, active when seen in the window.
people AS (
  SELECT COALESCE(n.workspace_name, k.workspace_id) AS workspace, k.who AS identity, k.kind, k.logins, k.queries,
         k.tables_read, k.tables_written, k.reads, k.writes, k.writes_nobody_reads, k.sensitive_tables,
         k.direct_grants, k.sensitive_grants,
         CAST(date_format(k.last_seen, 'yyyy-MM-dd') AS STRING) AS last_seen,
         datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(k.last_seen AS DATE)) AS days_since,
         CASE WHEN datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(k.last_seen AS DATE)) < 30 THEN 'Under 30 days'
              WHEN datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(k.last_seen AS DATE)) < 90 THEN '30-90 days'
              WHEN datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(k.last_seen AS DATE)) < 180 THEN '90-180 days'
              ELSE '180+ days' END AS last_seen_band,
         CASE WHEN datediff(date_add(:time_range.max, 1), CAST(k.last_seen AS DATE)) >= 90 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS inactive,
         CASE WHEN k.seen_in_window > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS active,
         CASE WHEN k.sensitive_tables > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS touched_sensitive,
         CASE WHEN k.kind = 'Person' AND k.seen_in_window > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS active_person,
         CASE WHEN k.kind = 'Service principal' AND k.seen_in_window > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS active_sp,
         1 AS identities
  FROM k
  LEFT JOIN wsl n ON n.workspace_id = k.workspace_id
  WHERE k.kind <> 'Other' AND k.workspace_id IS NOT NULL AND k.workspace_id <> '0'
)
                                        15. b: Every row of wd_people.
                                          Its SQL (8 lines)
                                          -- Every row of wd_people.
b AS (
SELECT workspace, identity, kind, logins, queries, tables_read, tables_written, reads, writes, writes_nobody_reads,
       sensitive_tables, direct_grants, sensitive_grants, last_seen, days_since, last_seen_band, inactive, active,
       touched_sensitive, active_person, active_sp, identities
FROM people
ORDER BY writes DESC, reads DESC, queries DESC, identity
)
                                        16. r: Ranked by read plus write events.
                                          Its SQL (6 lines)
                                          -- Ranked by read plus write events.
r AS (
  SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY reads + writes DESC, identity) AS rnk FROM b WHERE reads + writes > 0
)
SELECT workspace, identity AS name, kind, 'Reads' AS direction, reads AS events FROM r WHERE rnk <= 15
UNION ALL
